## 18. Addendum – QA investigation of 2026-11-19 (new findings, static review only)

> **Method and honesty statement.** This addendum records a **static** review of the working tree (branch `Feat-CheckInCheckOut`, HEAD `dae9154`, one stash, 247 uncommitted entries) plus read-only `git show`/`git ls-tree` of the fetched upstream ref `origin/Feat-CheckInCheckOut` (`2225865`, seven commits ahead: the merged search feature, PRs #80/#81). **No test, service, database, Keycloak or application was run in this investigation, so no result below is a test result.** Four read-only reviewer sub-agents covered (1) ownership, relationship, comment, pin, bookmark, retention and department; (2) signature, workflow, checkout, promotion, recycle bin and WOPI; (3) external sharing and document search; (4) the Next.js frontend. The lead auditor re-read the code behind every finding below that is marked CONFIRMED; findings marked AGENT-REPORTED were reported by a reviewer and have **not** been independently verified.
>
> **Classification legend.** **CONFIRMED (static)** – the lead auditor read the cited code and it proves the defect; not executed. **PROBABLE** – plausible from the code, needs runtime/concurrency verification. **DESIGN CONCERN** – works as written but is a weak design or a policy question. **AGENT-REPORTED** – reviewer claim, not independently re-read. **WIP** – affects uncommitted work in progress by another developer (the external-sharing feature); it is reported so it can be fixed before it ships, not as a defect in released code. Line numbers are as read on 2026-11-19 and will drift.
>
> **Blocked:** every runtime verification below is **BLOCKED – ENVIRONMENT UNAVAILABLE** (no PostgreSQL, Keycloak or running application was used).

### SEC-11 · `locked` and `disabled` users keep API access; caller status is never checked (High) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Component / evidence:** `user/UserServiceImpl.java` `updateUser` (status block, about lines 347-363): `UpdateUserRequest.Status` allows `active`, `locked`, `disabled`; the code sets the status and calls Keycloak `disableUser` **only** for `disabled` (and `enableUser` for `active`); `locked` touches nothing in Keycloak. `security/auth/TenantContextFilter.java:72-73` binds the tenant context for any user found by `findByKeycloakIdAndDeletedFalse` and never reads `user.getStatus()`. Status is read on the request path nowhere else: the only status checks are `ImpersonationContextFilter:117`, `ImpersonationService:78`, `JobIdentity:31` and target filters (mentions, ownership, import, notifications, escalation).
- **Contrast:** `deactivateUser` sets `deleted=true`, so the filter finds no row and the caller has no tenant context; the comment in `updateUser` ("a second route to the exact same outcome") is therefore not true.
- **Preconditions:** an administrator with the update-user permission; a target user with a valid session or token.
- **Reproduction:** `PUT /api/v1/users/{id}` with `status=locked`; the user's next API call with their existing token still succeeds. With `status=disabled`, Keycloak refuses new logins and refresh, but an access token already issued is still accepted by the DMS until it expires (30 min in the local realm: `infra/local/keycloak/oak-dms-realm.json:12`, `accessTokenLifespan: 1800`); Keycloak sessions are not explicitly revoked.
- **Expected:** a locked or disabled user is refused at once. **Actual (per code):** a locked user is unaffected; a disabled user keeps access for the token lifetime.
- **Impact:** an administrator who "locks" a compromised or departing account believes it is blocked when it is not. **Root cause:** status is stored but never enforced on the request path.
- **Remediation:** refuse non-active callers in `TenantContextFilter` (or make `locked` also disable the Keycloak account and revoke sessions). **Tests:** a request with a valid token for a `locked` and for a `disabled` user must return 403; no existing test covers this (only `ImportRowValidatorTest` uses `locked`). **Evidence gap:** not run against a live Keycloak.

### DOC-21 · Standalone signature placement removal has no document-access check and reverts the current version (High) – CONFIRMED (static); consequences AGENT-REPORTED
- **Component / evidence:** `signature/service/StandaloneSignaturePlacementService.java`: `resolve` (lines 265-268) looks the document up by id and tenant only; `remove` (about lines 199-219) calls `lockService.guard` and `editGuard.guard`, then sets `document.setCurrentVersionId(baseline)` and clears the base pin, with no `DocumentAccessService` call and no signed-version guard. The endpoint `DELETE /api/v1/documents/{documentId}/signature-placement` (`StandaloneSignatureController`) is gated only by the global `DSI_SIGN_DOCUMENT_STANDALONE` plus `DOCM_DOCUMENTS_CREATE`.
- **Preconditions:** a user holding those two permissions but no access to the document's folder; a document that already has a standalone placement.
- **Reproduction:** call the endpoint for such a document; the current version reverts to the pre-signature baseline.
- **Expected:** removal needs edit access to the document and is refused while the current version is signed. **Actual:** neither is checked. **Impact:** a tenant member without folder access can change which version of a document is current, and (reviewer-reported, not re-traced) can move a document out of the protection that `SignedContentGuard`/DOC-05 gives a signed current version, so upload, restore and the editor become possible again. **Root cause:** the standalone path was not given the access and signed-version checks that upload and restore have.
- **AGENT-REPORTED (not independently verified):** `place` and `addPage` also never call `SignedContentGuard`, only `contentReader.open` (view access); `signInternal` never clears the base-version pin.
- **Remediation:** require edit access on every method, refuse `place`/`addPage`/`remove` when the current version is signed, clear the pin after signing. **Tests:** a user without folder access calls `remove` and expects 404/403; none exists (`DocumentCheckoutIT` covers standalone `addPage` only).
- **Relationship:** incomplete coverage of the DOC-05 intent, found with new evidence; DOC-05 itself is not reopened.

### DOC-22 · Workflow submission and signature placement ignore signature lock, legal hold and check-out (Medium) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Evidence:** `workflow/service/impl/WorkflowSubmissionServiceImpl.java:88-111` locks the document and requires only `view` access, template, status and "no in-progress instance"; it never reads `getSignatureLockedAt()`, `isLegalHold()` or the check-out. `document/DocumentVersionPromoter.java:299-301` (`requirePromotable`) returns immediately when the scan's reason is `signed_copy`, so the lock, hold, signed-by-record and check-out checks are skipped for a signature-placement version. The promoter's own comment (about lines 295-297) already records a missing workflow-placement hold guard as a FOLLOW-UP, so the hold part is **partly known**; the signature-lock and check-out consequences are not mentioned there.
- **Reproduction (code-derived):** a signature-locked or held document is submitted again; an approver completes signature placement; a new current version is added where every other path returns `DOCUMENT_SIGNATURE_LOCKED` / `DOCUMENT_LEGAL_HOLD`. A check-in by another user is then refused because the current version is a `signed_copy`.
- **Expected:** submission and placement refuse a locked, held or foreign-checked-out document, as the standalone path does. **Impact:** document-integrity and legal-hold bypass through the workflow path. **Remediation:** the same guards at submission and at placement. **Tests:** none for workflow placement. **AGENT-REPORTED, not verified:** workflow placement takes no row lock on the task or instance, and does not re-check instance status.

### DOC-23 · Stale detached `Document` written after a synchronous scan (Medium) – PROBABLE; AGENT-REPORTED
- **Evidence (reviewer):** `StandaloneSignaturePlacementService` has no `@Transactional`; it loads a `Document`, reads, stamps and calls `ingestionGate.submit` (a synchronous scan), then `advanceDocument`/`remove` call `documentRepository.save(document)` on that detached entity; `Document` has `@DynamicUpdate` but no `@Version`. A legal hold or check-out set during the scan could be overwritten with the stale value.
- **Remediation:** re-read under `lockByDocumentIdAndTenantId` and re-run the guards before writing. **Tests:** none. **Evidence gap:** needs a database test with a hold placed during a slow scan.

### DOC-24 · `latestVersionId` and `currentVersionId` diverge after a standalone signature removal (Medium) – first half CONFIRMED (static); consequence PROBABLE
- **Evidence:** `remove` sets only `currentVersionId` back to the baseline (verified), while the promoter set both fields when the stamped version was created. **AGENT-REPORTED:** `WorkflowSubmissionServiceImpl.start` pins `getLatestVersionId()` as the version under approval, while signature code and readers use `currentVersionId`.
- **Consequence (probable, not run):** after a removal, an approval may be pinned to a different version from the one users see. **Remediation:** one definition of "the version", or repoint both fields. **Tests:** none.

### DOC-25 · WOPI access token is not re-authorised against user status or access for up to 10 hours (Medium) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Evidence:** `editing/WopiController.java:226-238` (`authorize`) verifies the HMAC token, the document id and the tenant's editing switch, then binds `TenantContext`; `canWriteNow` (about lines 246-260) checks signature lock, hold and check-out but not the user's status or folder access. Token lifetime defaults to `PT10H` (`editing/EditingSessionService.java:73`, `dms.editing.token-ttl`); `DocumentContentReader` deliberately skips the access check for editing reads (reviewer-reported).
- **Reproduction (code-derived):** open an editing session, then deactivate the user or remove their folder access; `GET`/`POST /contents` keep working until the token expires, and saves are attributed to that user. **Impact:** access revocation does not take effect for an open editor. **Remediation:** re-check user status and edit/view access on each WOPI call. **Tests:** not verified whether `WopiEditingIT` covers revocation (assumed not).

### DOC-26 · Retention version restore has no document-access check, and can race the file-destroy step (Medium) – access gap CONFIRMED (static); race PROBABLE
- **Evidence:** `compliance/VersionRetentionService.java` `restore` (lines 198-216) selects the version by id and tenant and un-hides it without calling `DocumentAccessService`, whereas the `removed()` listing filters with `canSee`. In `destroyDue` (lines 142-148) the file destruction runs first, then `update document_versions set purged_at = now() where version_id = ? and purged_at is null`, which does not re-check `retention_removed_at`, `is_protected` or legal hold, while `restore` updates rows `where … purged_at is null`.
- **Preconditions:** holder of `CRM_REVIEW_RETENTION`; for the race, a restore committing while `destroyRetentionDue` is deleting that version's files. **Impact:** a restricted document's version can be restored by someone who cannot see it; in the race a version ends visible and "kept" with its files already destroyed (irreversible). **Remediation:** check access in `restore`; make the final update conditional or claim the row first (`for update skip locked`). **Tests:** `VersionRetentionAndNotificationsIT` covers the normal path only. **Evidence gap:** the race needs a runtime concurrency test.

### DOC-27 · A mentioned user without document access also receives the parent comment (Low/Medium) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Evidence:** `comment/service/impl/DocumentCommentServiceImpl.java:171-199` (`get`): when the caller is mentioned in the requested comment the document access check is skipped (lines 180-188, decision 10), then the parent comment is loaded by id and tenant (lines 189-198) and returned with its body and author.
- **Impact:** decision 10 intends that a mentioned user reads the comment they were mentioned in, not the whole thread. **Remediation:** return the parent only if the caller has normal view access or is mentioned in it. **Tests:** none found.

### DOC-28 · Signature history and evidence endpoints have no per-document access check (Low) – AGENT-REPORTED, not independently verified
- **Evidence (reviewer):** `signature/controller/DocumentSignatureController.java` `list`, `verifyAll` and `evidence` are gated only by the global `DSI_VIEW_SIGNATURE_HISTORY`; `DocumentSignatureService.evidence` resolves by tenant only and returns signer name and email, comment, manifest and file hash; `verifyAll` also writes state. **Remediation:** document-access check. **Tests:** `DocumentSignatureServiceTest` is unit-level only.

### SEC-12 · `/me/provision` can recreate a local user for a still-valid token after a hard purge (Medium) – PROBABLE; design dependency of DB-01
- **Evidence:** `user/UserController.java` (`POST /api/v1/users/me/provision`, about lines 41-56) builds the request from the caller's own JWT claims (`sub`, `tenant_id`, `email`, `preferred_username`); `UserServiceImpl.provisionCurrentUser` (about lines 72-80) refuses only when `findByKeycloakIdAndDeletedFalse` finds a row. Today `uq_users_keycloak_id` (`V1__init_core_schema.sql:42`) makes a soft-deleted user's attempt fail, so the path is only open once the row is **hard-deleted**.
- **Consequence:** with the durable-deletion design for DB-01 (or any hard purge) a token issued before the purge can recreate an active local user for the same `keycloak_id` until it expires. **Remediation:** refuse a `keycloak_id` that has any deletion record, and confirm the Keycloak account is enabled. **Tests:** none. **Evidence gap:** needs Keycloak; not run.

### AUD-04 · Ownership-history CSV export writes no audit event (Low/Medium) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Evidence:** `ownership/service/OwnershipHistoryService.java` (`export`, about lines 94-152) streams up to 100,000 rows of document titles, owner names and notes; the class does not reference `AuditService`, unlike `EXPORT_REQUESTED`/`EXPORT_DOWNLOADED`/`AUDIT_LOG_EXPORTED` elsewhere (`AuditActionCatalog`). **Preconditions:** `DOT_HISTORY_EXPORT`. **Remediation:** emit an audit event with the filter and row count. **Tests:** `OwnershipHistoryIT` covers content only.

### DB-08 · Duplicate Flyway versions between the working tree and upstream; DB-01 migration number occupied (High, merge blocker) – CONFIRMED (read-only git)
- **Evidence:** the working tree has three **untracked** migrations, `V138__create_external_sharing.sql` (another developer), `V139__outbox_next_attempt_at.sql` (OBX-03) and `V140__outbox_published_cleanup_index.sql` (OBX-04). `origin/Feat-CheckInCheckOut` already contains **different** `V138__search_index_foundation`, `V139__grant_search_permission_to_document_viewers`, `V140__create_saved_searches`, `V141__grant_preset_permissions_to_search_users`, `V142__search_tenant_reactivation`, `V143__search_admin_run_state` (compare `git ls-tree` of the migration directory). The working tree itself has no duplicate versions (only the existing V35 gap).
- **Impact:** Flyway rejects duplicate versions at start-up; a database that already ran a local V138/V139/V140 cannot be fixed by renumbering alone. The version proposed for the DB-01 durable-deletion migration (V141) is occupied; **V144 is the next free number as of this review and must be re-checked at integration time.** The three local migrations also have no rollback scripts (upstream ships `db/rollback/*.rollback.sql` for its recent migrations).
- **Evidence gap:** whether any development database already applied the local versions is **unverified** (no database was reachable; the development database host was not usable). **Remediation:** renumber the untracked migrations above V143 before merge, with an approved plan for databases that applied them. This item records the risk only.
- **Working-tree resolution (2026-11-19, uncommitted; status of DB-08 unchanged):** the upstream branch `origin/Feat-CheckInCheckOut` (7 commits, PRs #80/#81) was applied to the working tree as uncommitted changes (HEAD stays `dae9154`, nothing staged or committed; every one of the 262 uncommitted files was first copied to `verification\backup_pre_upstream_20261119`), the six files edited on both sides were merged three-way, and the three untracked local migrations were **renamed with their SQL content unchanged**: `V138__create_external_sharing.sql` to `V144__create_external_sharing.sql` (another developer's sharing work), `V139__outbox_next_attempt_at.sql` to `V145__outbox_next_attempt_at.sql` (OBX-03) and `V140__outbox_published_cleanup_index.sql` to `V146__outbox_published_cleanup_index.sql` (OBX-04). The two OBX migration tests were renamed and their version logic updated (`V145OutboxNextAttemptMigrationTest`, `V146OutboxPublishedCleanupIndexMigrationTest`; Flyway targets 144/145/146), and the V138 comments in the sharing files now say V144. No duplicate Flyway versions remain in the working tree. A comment in `V146__outbox_published_cleanup_index.sql` still names the old test class (the SQL was deliberately not edited). `mvnw test-compile` of the integrated tree succeeded and the frontend `tsc --noEmit` reported 0 errors; no integration or migration test was run against the renumbered migrations, and no database was started. **Still open:** a database that already ran a local V138/V139/V140 under the old numbers will fail Flyway validation (history unverified, no database inspected), and none of this is committed.

### SHR-01 · A `restricted` document inside a shared folder is served to recipients (Medium/High) – WIP; CONFIRMED (static)
- **Affects uncommitted work in progress** (`sharing/*`, `V138__create_external_sharing.sql`, another developer).
- **Evidence:** a grep of the `sharing/` package finds no reference to `restricted` or `accessMode`, while `DocumentAccessService` has a per-document `restricted` mode that overrides the folder. `sharing/ShareTargets.java` (`servableBelow(Document …)`, about lines 262-265) allows a document that passes `retentionOk`, is not confidential and not in a confidential sub-folder; `ShareBrowser`/`PublicShareService`/`PortalService` list and serve on that basis.
- **Reproduction (code-derived):** a user with edit on folder F shares F; F contains a document restricted away from that user; the recipient lists and opens it. **Expected:** the sharer cannot expose what they cannot open. **Remediation:** skip restricted documents unless the sharer is an administrator or holds a document grant, and apply the same rule at serve time. **Tests:** `ExternalSharingIT` covers folder edit access, not restricted documents.

### SHR-02 · Guest sign-in attempt limit is not atomic; no throttle; "locked" reveals guest accounts (Medium) – WIP; code CONFIRMED, race exploitation PROBABLE
- **Affects uncommitted work in progress.** **Evidence:** `sharing/PortalService.java:100-156` (`login`) reads the guest, checks `lockedUntil`, verifies the password, then does a non-atomic `failedAttempts + 1; save`; share-link `open` takes a pessimistic lock but the portal path does not. No rate limiter exists under `sharing/` or for `/api/v1/public/**` in `SecurityConfig`. For a locked guest the loop skips the password check and the response is `locked`, while an unknown email returns `invalid` (lines 115-118, 140-141), so a run of wrong passwords reveals that a guest account exists, and anyone can lock a guest out.
- **Remediation:** lock the row or increment atomically, add per-IP and global limits, return a generic failure. **Tests:** the existing lockout test is sequential only. **Evidence gap:** parallel exploitation not run.

### SHR-03 · Public and portal content skip download and preview watermark policies (Medium) – WIP; DESIGN CONCERN, AGENT-REPORTED
- **Affects uncommitted work.** Reviewer: `document/SharedContentReader.java` (`original`, `viewable`) and `DocumentRenditionService.previewPdfForShare` (about lines 254-266) return the stored bytes or a plain rendition, whereas authenticated paths apply `defaultDownloadWatermark` and the preview watermark policy. Any change must keep serving existing signed or watermarked bytes untouched (watermark/signature rule). Not independently verified.

### SHR-04 · Links and grants stay live after the creator is deactivated or loses access (Low) – WIP; DESIGN CONCERN, AGENT-REPORTED
- **Affects uncommitted work.** Reviewer: `liveLink`, `usableTarget` and `reaches` never check that `createdBy` is still active or still has edit on the target; `ShareBrowser.serve` runs as that user. Links last until expiry (up to 90 days by default policy). Not independently verified. Note for DB-01: V138 `share_links.created_by` is a bare NOT NULL foreign key to `users`.

### FE-M5 · Workflow "Approve" is offered without `dsi.sign-documents.sign` (Medium) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Evidence:** `frontend/components/workflow/workflow-status-panel.tsx:103` (`canApprove = hasPermission(WA_TASK_APPROVE)`) and lines 238-243 open the sign dialog when `myStage?.requiresSignature`; `frontend/app/tasks/tasks-content.tsx` (about lines 66 and 172) does the same. The backend `workflow/controller/WorkflowTaskController.java:102-103` requires `WA_TASK_APPROVE` **and** `DSI_SIGN` for `/sign-and-approve`, and plain `/approve` on a signature stage returns 409 `WORKFLOW_SIGNATURE_REQUIRED`. `PermissionCodes.DSI_SIGN` is not used outside the code list (only the standalone signing code is used in `document-signatures-panel.tsx:63`).
- **Impact:** a user with approve but not sign permission completes the whole consent and password steps and then gets a 403 and cannot approve. **Remediation:** gate or explain in the UI. **Tests:** no vitest references `DSI_SIGN`.

### FE-L5 · Organisation-inactive handler never resets its dedupe flag (Low/Medium) – CONFIRMED (static)
- **Evidence:** `frontend/components/providers/providers.tsx` (`handleGlobalError`, about lines 79-114): the organisation-inactive branch sets `sessionExpiredHandled = true` and navigates with `router.push`, but unlike the session-expired branch has no `setTimeout` to reset it. After a client-side sign-in again, a later session expiry is ignored by both branches (`if (sessionExpiredHandled) return`), leaving the page broken until a reload. **Remediation:** reuse the reset timer. **Tests:** none for `providers.tsx`.

### Further candidate observations – NOT registered (AGENT-REPORTED or unverified; recorded so they are not lost)
- Ownership transfer with `grant=true` by a department head who only has **view** on the document may grant manage rights (`ownership/service/OwnershipTransferService` `checkVisibleAndInScope`, `OwnershipAccessService.apply`) – PROBABLE, not verified.
- Soft delete (`DocumentServiceImpl.deleteDocument`) takes no row lock and can race workflow submission or a legal hold – PROBABLE.
- Department delete/assign/cycle-check are check-then-act without a row lock – PROBABLE, Low.
- Standalone `signStandalone` ignores another user's check-out (can discard unsaved work) – AGENT-REPORTED.
- `LegalHoldService.place` cannot hold a document that is in the recycle bin – AGENT-REPORTED.
- Retention scopes endpoint lists all folder names regardless of folder access (`RetentionPolicyService.scopes`) – AGENT-REPORTED.
- Search (upstream, not in the working tree): a "refine" notice can reveal that many hidden documents match (Low); `/search/filters` returns the tenant's user directory by design; the guest portal token lives in `sessionStorage` with no revoke endpoint – DESIGN CONCERNS.
- Sound as reviewed: tenant isolation in the modules read (no cross-tenant IDOR found), search tenant and visibility layers, share token generation and storage, relationship and pin and bookmark services, workflow task engine locking.

### Evidence gaps and coverage limits of this addendum
- **No runtime evidence:** no test or service was run; all PROBABLE items and every concurrency claim need a disposable PostgreSQL and, for identity items, Keycloak.
- **Not independently verified by the lead:** DOC-23, DOC-28, SHR-03, SHR-04, the `place`/`addPage` and pin parts of DOC-21, the workflow-start half of DOC-24, the DOC-07 residual-gap list, and all items under "Further candidate observations".
- **Not read in full:** `RetentionNotifier`/`Resolver`, relationship-type admin, metadata type/field service internals, `WorkflowTemplateServiceImpl`, the workflow query service, notification/mailer classes, the signature stamper formats, OCR artifact writers and rendition services, the Python OCR worker.
- **Database history:** whether any development database ran the local V138/V139/V140 is unknown (see DB-08).
